Staging requires its own access key, supplied privately. Production requires its own key. Keys are excluded from this pack. No paid provider requests were made.

3 · Discountable totals — Miami-Dade additional check

Use the same Unit 512 input as issue 1. Dated 2025 source-derived annual-bill evidence reports $3,477.20 as Total Discountable. This differs from the $4,085.87 current payable quote and the $3,885.35 certificate face. Fresh staging returned TaxAmounts.totalDiscountable: null because collector access was blocked; production is NOT RUN. The issue 1 screenshots and staging-miami.json apply to this check too. Parser support is verified offline; full live resolution is pending.

Manager acceptance checklist

  1. Run the full-suffix and suffix-free Miami inputs. Both must resolve to folio 01-3231-067-0130, Unit 512 and the supported owner. Never accept a neighbour unit.
  2. For delinquency, verify TaxInformation.amountDue/type, AccountHistory billYear/billType/status/amountDue, DelinquencyInformation.hasActiveDelinquency/currentYearDue/delinquentYears[*].amountDue/code/certificate, totalPriorYearsDue, coverage and unavailableYears. Certificate face value is not a substitute for the current payoff quote.
  3. For discountable totals, verify TaxAmounts.totalDiscountable against an explicit source label on the same account and annual bill year. Also compare adValorem, nonAdValorem, totalTax, BillDetails and PaymentDetails; these are distinct values.
  4. For Bradford, obtain Besil’s affected address/parcel, year and source URL or screenshot. The Walmart control is not a replacement positive example. Null cannot be changed to a paid amount, discount or combined total.
  5. Check all returned sections: PropertyInformation; FullLegalDescription; AssessmentInformation; AssessmentHistory; BenefitsInformation/BenefitsNote; SalesInformation; TaxableValueInformation; LandBuildingExtraFeatures; TaxInformation; AccountHistory; BillDetails; ParcelDetails; AdValoremMillage; NonAdValorem/NonAdValoremNote; AdValoremGroupTotals; Exemptions/ExemptionsNote; TaxAmounts; PaymentDetails; DelinquencyInformation.
  6. Check ExtractionMetadata: request_id, collected_at, duration_ms, county_id, match, section_status, sources (URL, status, retrieval time, hash), provenance, errors and validation. Distinguish 2026 appraiser values from 2025 collector bills. Null means unavailable; zero must be source-supported.
  7. Negative matching tests (not live-run in this pack): conflicting Unit 513 vs explicit unit 512 must fail; wrong owner must not match silently; conflicting supplied suffix must not silently match; multiple candidates and incomplete pagination must stay explicit. These are criteria, not claims of new live passes.
  8. Once production budget/entitlement is verified, run the same inputs there within the existing $5 cap, record the current remaining budget, and replace the NOT RUN columns with downloaded JSON and fresh screenshots. Stop after two non-advancing attempts on a blocked source. Do not reset the budget.

What remains needed

Production current budget/entitlement preflight; original managed-source credentials and reconciled ledger/history for staging recovery; Besil’s Bradford positive example. The two additional states’ requirements are separate pending intake.

Supporting parser evidence

The same saved source-derived Miami excerpt produced no delinquent entry with the baseline parser and one $4,085.87 entry with the updated parser. It is not a live-production result. The local and staging release passed 306 automated checks; this does not establish every field for every property.
